      The correct answer is Hole mobility increases in the collector region(Option 1).

      \n\n

      Key Points

      \n\n
        \n
      • PNP Transistor is a type of bipolar junction transistor (BJT) where the majority charge carriers are holes.
        • \n
      • When the collector–base reverse bias increases, the electric field in the collector region strengthens.
        • \n
      • This enhanced electric field increases the mobility of the holes in the collector region slightly.
        • \n
      • Even when the emitter current is constant, this mobility increase leads to a small rise in collector current.
        • \n
      • The phenomenon is typically referred to as “Early effect”, which also involves base-width modulation.
        • \n
      \n\n

      Additional Information

      \n\n
        \n
      • Early Effect:\n
          \n
        • Also known as base-width modulation, it occurs when the effective width of the base decreases with an increase in collector–base reverse bias.
          • \n
        • This leads to an increase in the collector current, even under constant emitter current conditions.
          • \n
        \n
        • \n
      • Impact on Device Performance:\n
          \n
        • While the Early Effect can improve current gain slightly, it also introduces non-linearity in the transistor's performance.
          • \n
        • Designers account for this effect while creating circuits to ensure stable operation.
          • \n
        \n
        • \n

        The correct answer is four times(Option 1).

        \n\n

        Key Points

        \n\n
          \n
        • Power consumed in an electrical circuit is given by the formula P = V2/R.
          • \n
        • When the potential difference (V) is doubled, the power becomes P = (2V)2/R = 4V2/R.
          • \n
        • This means the power consumed becomes four times the original value.
          • \n
        • The resistance (R) remains constant, so any increase in voltage directly impacts the power.
          • \n
        \n\n

        Additional Information

        \n\n
          \n
        • Ohm's Law:\n
            \n
          • Ohm's law states that the current through a conductor between two points is directly proportional to the voltage across the two points.
            • \n
          • The formula is V = IR, where V is voltage, I is current, and R is resistance.
            • \n
          \n
          • \n
        • Power in Resistive Circuits:\n
            \n
          • The power dissipated in a resistor can also be expressed as P = I2R.
            • \n
          • This shows that power depends on both current and resistance in the circuit.
            • \n
          \n

            • \n
          • जब विभवांतर (V) को दोगुना कर दिया जाता है, तो शक्ति P = (2V)2/R = 4V2/R हो जाती है।
            • \n
          • इसका अर्थ है कि खपत की गई शक्ति मूल मान की चार गुना हो जाती है।
            • \n
          • प्रतिरोध (R) स्थिर रहता है, इसलिए वोल्टेज में कोई भी वृद्धि सीधे शक्ति को प्रभावित करती है।

              • \n

The ratio of their cross-sectional areas (\\( A_1:A_2 \\)) is _______.", "options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "<span>1:4</span>"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "<span>1:2</span>"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "<span>4:1</span>"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "<span>2:1</span>"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}, "hn": {"value": "समान पदार्थ के दो चालकों की लंबाई L और 2L है और उनका प्रतिरोध समान है। उनके अनुप्रस्थ काट के क्षेत्रफल का अनुपात (\\( A_1:A_2 \\)) _______ है।", "options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "1:4"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "1:2"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "4:1"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "2:1"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "2", "solution_html": {"en": "\n

            Concept:

            \n\n

            The resistance of a conductor is given by,

            \n\n

            \\(R=\\frac{\\rho L}{A}\\)

            \n\n

            where,

            \n\n

            R = resistance of the conductor

            \n\n

            ρ = resistivity of the material

            \n\n

            L = length of the conductor

            \n\n

            A = cross-sectional area of the conductor

            \n\n

            Calculation:

            \n\n

            Given:

            \n\n

            Length of first conductor = L

            \n\n

            Length of second conductor = 2L

            \n\n

            Resistance of both conductors is equal.

            \n\n

            For the first conductor,

            \n\n

            \\(R=\\frac{\\rho L}{A_1}\\)

            \n\n

            For the second conductor,

            \n\n

            \\(R=\\frac{\\rho (2L)}{A_2}\\)

            \n\n

            Since the resistances are equal,

            \n\n

            \\(\\frac{\\rho L}{A_1}=\\frac{\\rho (2L)}{A_2}\\)

            \n\n

            Cancelling ρ and L from both sides,

            \n\n

            \\(\\frac{1}{A_1}=\\frac{2}{A_2}\\)

            \n\n

            \\(A_2=2A_1\\)

            \n\n

            Therefore,

            \n\n

            \\(A_1:A_2=1:2\\)

            \n\n

            Answer:

            \n\n

            Option (2) : 1 : 2

            \n\n

            \n\n

            \\(R=\\frac{\\rho L}{A}\\)

            \n\n

            जहाँ,

            \n\n

            R = चालक का प्रतिरोध

            \n\n

            \n\n

            \n\n

            पहले चालक की लंबाई = L

            \\(A_2=2A_1\\)

            \n\n

            \n\n

            \\(A_1:A_2=1:2\\)

            When several resistors are connected in series, which physical quantity has an identical value across each resistor?", "options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "<span>Power</span>"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "<span>Voltage</span>"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "<span>Current</span>"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "<span>Resistance</span>"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}, "hn": {"value": "जब कई प्रतिरोधकों को शृंखला में जोड़ा जाता है, तो प्रत्येक प्रतिरोधक में कौन सी भौतिक मात्रा का मान समान होता है?", "options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "शक्ति"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "वोल्टेज"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "विद्युत धारा"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "प्रतिरोध"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "3", "solution_html": {"en": "\n

            The correct answer is Current(3).

            \n\n

            Key Points

            \n\n
              \n\t
            • When resistors are connected in series, the current passing through each resistor is the same.
              • \n\t
            • This is because in a series connection, there is only one path for the current to flow.
              • \n\t
            • In contrast, the voltage across each resistor in series varies based on their resistance values.
              • \n\t
            • The total resistance of the series combination is the sum of the individual resistances.
              • \n\t
            • Examples of series circuits include string lights where if one bulb fails, the entire circuit is broken.
              • \n
            \n\n

            Additional Information

            \n\n
              \n\t
            • Series vs Parallel Circuits:\n\n\t
                \n\t\t
              • In a series circuit, the current remains the same, but the voltage divides among components.
                • \n\t\t
              • In a parallel circuit, the voltage remains constant across all branches, but the current divides.
                • \n\t
              \n\t
              • \n\t
            • Ohm's Law:\n\t
                \n\t\t
              • Ohm's Law states that V = I × R, where V is voltage, I is current, and R is resistance.
                • \n\t\t
              • This fundamental principle helps analyze both series and parallel circuits.
                • \n\t
              \n\t
              • \n\t
            • Practical Applications:\n\t
                \n\t\t
              • Series circuits are used in applications where the current consistency is vital.
                • \n\t\t
              • Examples include batteries connected in series to increase voltage output.
                • \n\t
              \n\t
              • \n

              • \n\t

            • शृंखला परिपथ के उदाहरणों में Which of the following statements correctly describes weight?", "options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "<span>Weight is measured in kilograms</span>"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "<span>Weight has both magnitude and direction</span>"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "<span>Weight only shows how heavy something is</span>"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "<span>Weight has no direction</span>"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}, "hn": {"value": "निम्नलिखित में से कौन सा कथन भार का सही वर्णन करता है?", "options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "भार को किलोग्राम में मापा जाता है"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "भार में परिमाण और दिशा दोनों होते हैं"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "भार केवल यह दर्शाता है कि कोई वस्तु कितनी भारी है"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "भार की कोई दिशा नहीं होती है"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "2", "solution_html": {"en": "\n

              The correct answer is 2) Weight has both magnitude and direction.

              \n\n

              Concept:

              \n\n

              Weight is a vector quantity, which means it has both a magnitude (how strong the pull is) and a specific direction (pulling straight down toward the centre of gravity).

              \n\n
                \n\t
              • \n\t

                The Formula: Weight is the gravitational force acting on an object, calculated as: W = m.g

                \n\n\t

                Where m is mass and g is the acceleration due to gravity.

                \n\t
                • \n\t
              • \n\t

                Direction: Because gravity pulls objects toward the centre of the earth (or any planetary body), weight always acts downward.

                \n\t
                • \n
              \n ", "hn": "\n

              सही उत्तर 2) भार में परिमाण और दिशा दोनों होते हैं है।

              \n\n

              अवधारणा:

              \n\n

              भार एक सदिश राशि है, जिसका अर्थ है कि इसमें एक परिमाण (खिंचाव कितना मजबूत है) और एक विशिष्ट दिशा (गुरुत्वाकर्षण के केंद्र की ओर सीधे नीचे खींचना) दोनों होते हैं।

              \n\n
                \n\t
              • \n\t

                सूत्र: भार किसी वस्तु पर कार्य करने वाला गुरुत्वाकर्षण बल है, जिसकी गणना इस प्रकार की जाती है: W = m.g

                \n\n\t

                जहाँ m द्रव्यमान है और g गुरुत्वीय त्वरण है।

                \n\t
                • \n\t

              Concept:

              \n\n

              The RMS value of current in a resistor is given by,

              \n\n

              \\(I_{rms}=\\frac{V_{rms}}{R}\\)

              \n\n

              For a sinusoidal waveform,

              \n\n

              \\(V_{rms}=\\frac{V_p}{\\sqrt{2}}\\)

              \n\n

              In a CRO, the peak-to-peak voltage is obtained as,

              \n\n

              \\(V_{p-p}=(\\text{Number of divisions})\\times(\\text{Volts/div})\\)

              \n\n

              Calculation:

              \n\n

              Given:

              \n\n

              Resistance, संकल्पना:

              \n\n

              एक प्रतिरोधक में धारा का RMS मान निम्न द्वारा दिया जाता है,

              \n\n

              \\(I_{rms}=\\frac{V_{rms}}{R}\\)

              \n\n

              \n\n

              \n\n

              एक CRO में, पीक-टू-पीक वोल्टेज निम्न रूप में प्राप्त किया जाता है,

              \n\n

              \\(V_{p-p}=(\\text{विभाजनों की संख्या})\\times(\\text{Volts/div})\\)

              Concept:

              \n\n

              To implement a given logic function using a minimum number of basic gates, we can transform the expression using De Morgan's Laws to eliminate unsupported operations (like the OR operation) so that it only contains multiplications (AND) and inversions (NOT).

              \n\n

              De Morgan's Law states that:

              \n\n

              \\(\\overline{X + Y} = \\overline{X} \\cdot \\overline{Y}\\)

              \n\n

              For a function with multiple inputs, a double inversion is applied to keep the value identical while rewriting the operations:

              \n\n

              \\(F = \\overline{\\overline{F}}\\)

              \n\n

              Calculation:

              \n\n

              Given:

              \n\n

              Logic function: F = ABC + DE

              \n\n

              Allowed gates: Only 2-input AND gates and NOT gates.

              \n\n

              Step 1: Convert the function using De Morgan's Laws

              \n\n

              Apply double inversion to remove the OR operation:

              \n\n

              \\(F = \\overline{\\overline{ABC + DE}}\\)

              \n\n

              Applying De Morgan's Law inside the first inversion yields:

              \n\n

              \\(F = \\overline{(\\overline{ABC}) \\cdot (\\overline{DE})}\\)

              \n\n

              Step 2: Gate Counting Analysis

              \n\n

              1. Implementing the term \\(ABC\\) using 2-input AND gates:

              \n\n
                \n\t
              • First 2-input AND gate: \\(X = A \\cdot B\\)
                • \n\t
              • Second 2-input AND gate: \\(Y = X \\cdot C = ABC\\)
                • \n
              \n\n

              (Requires 2 AND gates)

              \n\n

              2. Implementing the term \\(DE\\) using a 2-input AND gate:

              \n\n
                \n\t
              • Third 2-input AND gate: \\(Z = D \\cdot E = DE\\)
                • \n
              \n\n

              (Requires 1 AND gate)

              \n\n

              3. Inverting the intermediate products:

              \n\n
                \n\t
              • First NOT gate: \\(\\overline{ABC}\\)
                • \n\t
              • Second NOT gate: \\(\\overline{DE}\\)
                • \n
              \n\n

              (Requires 2 NOT gates)

              \n\n

              4. Combining the inverted components with an AND operation:

              \n\n
                \n\t
              • Fourth 2-input AND gate: \\(W = (\\overline{ABC}) \\cdot (\\overline{DE})\\)
                • \n
              \n\n

              (Requires 1 AND gate)

              \n\n

              5. Performing the final external inversion:

              \n\n
                \n\t
              • Third NOT gate: \\(F = \\overline{W} = \\overline{(\\overline{ABC}) \\cdot (\\overline{DE})}\\)
                • \n
              \n\n

              (Requires 1 NOT gate)

              \n\n

              Conclusion:

              \n\n

              Total 2-input AND gates needed = 2 + 1 + 1 = 4 AND gates

              \n\n

              Total NOT gates needed = 2 + 1 = 3 NOT gates

              ", "hn": "\n

              अवधारणा:

              \n\n

              न्यूनतम संख्या में बेसिक गेट्स का उपयोग करके दिए गए लॉजिक फ़ंक्शन को लागू करने के लिए, हम असमर्थित संक्रियाओं (जैसे OR संक्रिया) को समाप्त करने के लिए डी मॉर्गन के नियमों (De Morgan's Laws) का उपयोग करके व्यंजक को बदल सकते हैं ताकि इसमें केवल गुणन (AND) और व्युत्क्रमण (NOT) शामिल हों।

              \n\n

              डी मॉर्गन का नियम कहता है कि:

              \n\n

              \\(\\overline{X + Y} = \\overline{X} \\cdot \\overline{Y}\\)

              \n\n

              एकाधिक इनपुट वाले फ़ंक्शन के लिए, संक्रियाओं को फिर से लिखते समय मान को समान रखने के लिए दोहरा व्युत्क्रमण (double inversion) लागू किया जाता है:

              \n\n

              \\(F = \\overline{\\overline{F}}\\)

              \n\n

              गणना:

              \n\n

              दिया गया है:

              \n\n

              लॉजिक फ़ंक्शन: F = ABC + DE

              \n\n

              अनुमत गेट: केवल 2-इनपुट AND गेट और NOT गेट।

              \n\n

              चरण 1: डी मॉर्गन के नियमों का उपयोग करके फ़ंक्शन को बदलें

              \n\n

              OR संक्रिया को हटाने के लिए दोहरा व्युत्क्रमण लागू करें:

              \n\n

              \\(F = \\overline{\\overline{ABC + DE}}\\)

              \n\n

              पहले व्युत्क्रमण के अंदर डी मॉर्गन का नियम लागू करने पर प्राप्त होता है:

              \n\n

              \\(F = \\overline{(\\overline{ABC}) \\cdot (\\overline{DE})}\\)

              \n\n

              चरण 2: गेट गणना विश्लेषण

              \n\n

              1. 2-इनपुट AND गेट का उपयोग करके पद \\(ABC\\) को लागू करना:

              \n\n
                \n\t
              • पहला 2-इनपुट AND गेट: \\(X = A \\cdot B\\)
                • \n\t
              • दूसरा 2-इनपुट AND गेट: \\(Y = X \\cdot C = ABC\\)
                • \n
              \n\n

              (2 AND गेट की आवश्यकता है)

              \n\n

              2. 2-इनपुट AND गेट का उपयोग करके पद \\(DE\\) को लागू करना:

              \n\n
                \n\t
              • तीसरा 2-इनपुट AND गेट: \\(Z = D \\cdot E = DE\\)
                • \n
              \n\n

              (1 AND गेट की आवश्यकता है)

              \n\n

              3. मध्यवर्ती गुणनफलों का व्युत्क्रमण:

              \n\n
                \n\t
              • पहला NOT गेट: \\(\\overline{ABC}\\)
                • \n\t
              • दूसरा NOT गेट: \\(\\overline{DE}\\)
                • \n
              \n\n

              (2 NOT गेट की आवश्यकता है)

              \n\n

              4. व्युत्क्रमित घटकों को AND संक्रिया के साथ संयोजित करना:

              \n\n
                \n\t
              • चौथा 2-इनपुट AND गेट: \\(W = (\\overline{ABC}) \\cdot (\\overline{DE})\\)
                • \n
              \n\n

              (1 AND गेट की आवश्यकता है)

              \n\n

              5. अंतिम बाहरी व्युत्क्रमण करना:

              \n\n
                \n\t
              • तीसरा NOT गेट: \\(F = \\overline{W} = \\overline{(\\overline{ABC}) \\cdot (\\overline{DE})}\\)
                • \n
              \n\n

              (1 NOT गेट की आवश्यकता है)

              \n\n

              निष्कर्ष:

              \n\n

              आवश्यक कुल 2-इनपुट AND गेट = 2 + 1 + 1 = 4 AND गेट

              \n\n

              आवश्यक कुल NOT गेट = 2 + 1 = 3 NOT गेट

              Key Points

                \n\t
              • The formula for magnetic flux is given by Φ = B × A × cos(θ), where:
                • \n\t
              • B is the magnetic field, A is the area of the loop, and θ is the angle between the field and the normal to the loop.
                • \n\t
              • For a circular loop, the area is A = πr², where r is the radius of the loop.
                • \n\t
              • If the plane of the loop is perpendicular to the magnetic field, then θ = 0°, and cos(θ) = 1.

                The correct answer is 9.8 m/s²(Option 2).

                \n\n

                Key Points

                \n\n
                  \n\t
                • The gravitational force on an object is given by the formula F = m × g.
                  • \n\t
                • Here, F = 49 N and m = 5 kg, so g = F/m = 49/5 = 9.8 m/s².
                  • \n\t
                • This value of g corresponds to the acceleration due to gravity on the surface of the Earth.
                  • \n\t
                • The acceleration due to gravity varies slightly depending on location but is commonly approximated as 9.8 m/s².
                  • \n\t
                • This value is used to calculate gravitational force in various physics problems.
                  • \n
                \n\n

                Additional Information

                \n\n
                  \n\t
                • Acceleration due to gravity:\n\n\t
                    \n\t\t
                  • It is the rate of increase of velocity of an object due to the Earth's gravitational force.
                    • \n\t\t
                  • It is denoted by g and has a standard value of 9.8 m/s².
                    • \n\t
                  \n\t
                  • \n\t
                • Variation of g:\n\t
                    \n\t\t
                  • g decreases with altitude and increases towards the poles due to the Earth's shape.
                    • \n\t\t
                  • It also depends on the density of the Earth's crust in the location.
                    • \n\t
                  \n\t
                  • \n\t
                • Weight of an object:\n\t
                    \n\t\t
                  • Weight is the product of mass (m) and acceleration due to gravity (g).
                    • \n\t\t
                  • It is a force and is measured in newtons (N).
                    • \n\t
                  \n\t
                  • \n
                ", "hn": "\n

                सही उत्तर 9.8 m/s²(विकल्प 2) है।

                \n\n

                Key Points

                \n\n
                  \n\t
                • किसी वस्तु पर गुरुत्वाकर्षण बल सूत्र F = m × g द्वारा दिया जाता है।
                  • \n\t
                • यहाँ, F = 49 N और m = 5 kg, इसलिए g = F/m = 49/5 = 9.8 m/s²।
                  • \n\t
                • g का यह मान पृथ्वी की सतह पर गुरुत्वीय त्वरण से मेल खाता है।
                  • \n\t
                • गुरुत्वीय त्वरण स्थान के आधार पर थोड़ा भिन्न होता है लेकिन आमतौर पर इसे 9.8 m/s² के रूप में अनुमानित किया जाता है।
                  • \n\t
                • इस मान का उपयोग विभिन्न भौतिकी समस्याओं में गुरुत्वाकर्षण बल की गणना करने के लिए किया जाता है।
                  • \n
                \n\n

                Additional Information

                \n\n
                  \n\t
                • गुरुत्वीय त्वरण:\n\n\t
                    \n\t\t
                  • यह पृथ्वी के गुरुत्वाकर्षण बल के कारण किसी वस्तु के वेग में वृद्धि की दर है।
                    • \n\t\t
                  • इसे g द्वारा निरूपित किया जाता है और इसका मानक मान 9.8 m/s² है।
                    • \n\t
                  \n\t
                  • \n\t
                • g में भिन्नता:\n\t
                    \n\t\t
                  • पृथ्वी के आकार के कारण g ऊंचाई के साथ घटता है और ध्रुवों की ओर बढ़ता है।
                    • \n\t\t
                  • यह स्थान में पृथ्वी की पपड़ी (क्रस्ट) के घनत्व पर भी निर्भर करता है।
                    • \n\t
                  \n\t
                  • \n\t
                • किसी वस्तु का भार:\n\t
                    \n\t\t
                  • भार द्रव्यमान (m) और गुरुत्वीय त्वरण (g) का गुणनफल है।
                    • \n\t\t
                  • यह एक बल है और इसे न्यूटन (N) में मापा जाता है।
                    • \n\t
                  \n\t

                  సరైన సమాధానం 9.8 m/s²(ఆప్షన్ 2).

                    • \n\t

                    • \n\t

                    • \n\t

                      • \n\t\t

                      \n\t\t
                    • ఎత్తు పెరిగేకొద్దీ g తగ్గుతుంది మరియు భూమి ఆకారం కారణంగా ధ్రువాల వైపు పెరుగుతుంది.

                  \n\n9b:Te4b,\n

                  योग्य उत्तर 9.8 m/s²(पर्याय 2). आहे.

                  \n\n

                  Key Points

                  \n\n
                    \n\t
                  • एखाद्या वस्तूवरील गुरुत्वीय बल F = m × g या सूत्राने दिले जाते.
                    • \n\t
                  • येथे, F = 49 N आणि m = 5 kg, म्हणून g = F/m = 49/5 = 9.8 m/s².
                    • \n\t
                  • g चे हे मूल्य पृथ्वीच्या पृष्ठभागावरील गुरुत्वत्वरणाशी सुसंगत आहे.
                    • \n\t
                  • गुरुत्वत्वरणाचे मूल्य स्थानानुसार किंचित बदलते परंतु सामान्यतः ते 9.8 m/s² असे अंदाजित केले जाते.
                    • \n\t
                  • या मूल्याचा उपयोग विविध भौतिकशास्त्रविषयक समस्यांमध्ये गुरुत्वीय बल मोजण्यासाठी केला जातो.
                    • \n
                  \n\n

                  योग्य उत्तर 9.8 m/s²(पर्याय 2). आहे.

                  \n\n

                  Key Points

                  \n\n
                    \n\t
                  • एखाद्या वस्तूवरील गुरुत्वीय बल F = m × g या सूत्राने दिले जाते.
                    • \n\t
                  • येथे, F = 49 N आणि m = 5 kg, म्हणून g = F/m = 49/5 = 9.8 m/s².
                    • \n\t
                  • g चे हे मूल्य पृथ्वीच्या पृष्ठभागावरील गुरुत्वत्वरणाशी सुसंगत आहे.
                    • \n\t
                  • गुरुत्वत्वरणाचे मूल्य स्थानानुसार किंचित बदलते परंतु सामान्यतः ते 9.8 m/s² असे अंदाजित केले जाते.
                    • \n\t
                  • या मूल्याचा उपयोग विविध भौतिकशास्त्रविषयक समस्यांमध्ये गुरुत्वीय बल मोजण्यासाठी केला जातो.
                    • \n
                  \n\n

                  Additional Information

                  \n\n
                    \n\t
                  • गुरुत्वत्वरण (Acceleration due to gravity):\n\n\t
                      \n\t\t
                    • पृथ्वीच्या गुरुत्वीय बलामुळे वस्तूच्या वेगामध्ये होणाऱ्या वाढीचा हा दर आहे.
                      • \n\t\t
                    • हे g ने दर्शवले जाते आणि त्याचे प्रमाणित मूल्य 9.8 m/s² आहे.
                      • \n\t
                    \n\t
                    • \n\t
                  • g मधील बदल:\n\t
                      \n\t\t
                    • उंचीनुसार g कमी होतो आणि पृथ्वीच्या आकारामुळे ध्रुवांकडे वाढत जातो.
                      • \n\t\t
                    • हे त्या ठिकाणच्या पृथ्वीच्या कवचाच्या घनतेवर देखील अवलंबून असते.
                      • \n\t
                    \n\t
                    • \n\t
                  • वस्तूचे वजन:\n\t
                      \n\t\t
                    • वजन म्हणजे वस्तुमान (m) आणि गुरुत्वत्वरण (g) यांचा गुणाकार होय.
                      • \n\t\t
                    • हे एक बल असून ते न्यूटन (N) मध्ये मोजले जाते.

                  \n\n9c:Te79,\n

                  সঠিক উত্তরটি হলো 9.8 m/s²(বিকল্প 2)।

                  \n\n

                  Key Points

                  \n\n
                    \n\t
                  • একটি বস্তুর ওপর প্রযুক্ত মহাকর্ষীয় বল F = m × g সূত্র দ্বারা নির্ধারিত হয়।
                    • \n\t
                  • এখানে, F = 49 N এবং m = 5 kg, তাই g = F/m = 49/5 = 9.8 m/s²।
                    • \n\t
                  • g-এর এই মানটি পৃথিবীর পৃষ্ঠে অভিকর্ষজ ত্বরণের সাথে মিলে যায়।
                    • \n\t

                    সঠিক উত্তরটি হলো 9.8 m/s²(বিকল্প 2)।

                    Key Points

                    \n\n
                      \n\t
                    • একটি বস্তুর ওপর প্রযুক্ত মহাকর্ষীয় বল F = m × g সূত্র দ্বারা নির্ধারিত হয়।
                      • \n\t
                    • এখানে, F = 49 N এবং m = 5 kg, তাই g = F/m = 49/5 = 9.8 m/s²।
                      • \n\t
                    • g-এর এই মানটি পৃথিবীর পৃষ্ঠে অভিকর্ষজ ত্বরণের সাথে মিলে যায়।
                      • \n\t
                    • অবস্থানের ওপর ভিত্তি করে অভিকর্ষজ ত্বরণ বা g-এর মান সামান্য পরিবর্তিত হয়, তবে সাধারণত এটি 9.8 m/s² হিসেবে ধরা হয়।
                      • \n\t
                    • পদার্থবিজ্ঞানের বিভিন্ন সমস্যায় মহাকর্ষীয় বল নির্ণয় করতে এই মানটি ব্যবহৃত হয়।

                    Additional Information

                    \n\n

                        \n\t\t
                      • পৃথিবীর মহাকর্ষীয় বলের প্রভাবে কোনো বস্তুর বেগের বৃদ্ধির হারকে অভিকর্ষজ ত্বরণ বলা হয়।
                        • \n\t\t
                      • এটি g দ্বারা চিহ্নিত করা হয় এবং এর আদর্শ মান হলো 9.8 m/s²।
                        • \n\t
                      \n\t
                      • \n\t
                    • g-এর মানের পরিবর্তন:\n\t
                        \n\t\t
                      • উচ্চতা বাড়ার সাথে সাথে g-এর মান কমে এবং পৃথিবীর আকৃতির কারণে মেরু অঞ্চলের দিকে এটি বৃদ্ধি পায়।
                        • \n\t\t

                        • \n\t
                      \n\t
                      • \n\t
                    • বস্তুর ওজন:\n\t
                        \n\t\t
                      • ওজন হলো ভর (m) এবং অভিকর্ষজ ত্বরণ (g)-এর গুণফল।
                        • \n\t\t
                      • এটি একটি বল এবং এটি নিউটন (N) এককে পরিমাপ করা হয়।
                        • \n\t

                    \n\n9d:Tec0,\n

                    સાચો જવાબ 9.8 m/s²(વિકલ્પ 2) છે.

                    \n\n

                    Key Points

                    \n\n
                      \n\t
                    • વસ્તુ પર લાગતું ગુરુત્વાકર્ષણ બળ F = m × g સૂત્ર દ્વારા આપવામાં આવે છે.
                      • \n\t
                    • અહીં, F = 49 N અને m = 5 kg, તેથી g = F/m = 49/5 = 9.8 m/s².
                      • \n\t
                    • g નું આ મૂલ્ય પૃથ્વીની સપાટી પર ગુરુત્વાકર્ષણને કારણે થતા પ્રવેગને અનુરૂપ છે.
                      • \n\t
                    • ગુરુત્વાકર્ષણને કારણે થતો પ્રવેગ g સ્થાનના આધારે થોડો બદલાય છે પરંતુ સામાન્ય રીતે અંદાજે 9.8 m/s² લેવામાં આવે છે.
                      • \n\t
                    • આ મૂલ્યનો ઉપયોગ ભૌતિક વિજ્ઞાનના વિવિધ પ્રશ્નોમાં ગુરુત્વાકર્ષણ બળની ગણતરી કરવા માટે થાય છે.
                      • \n
                    \n\n

                    સાચો જવાબ 9.8 m/s²(વિકલ્પ 2) છે.

                    \n\n

                    Key Points

                    \n\n
                      \n\t
                    • વસ્તુ પર લાગતું ગુરુત્વાકર્ષણ બળ F = m × g સૂત્ર દ્વારા આપવામાં આવે છે.
                      • \n\t
                    • અહીં, F = 49 N અને m = 5 kg, તેથી g = F/m = 49/5 = 9.8 m/s².
                      • \n\t
                    • g નું આ મૂલ્ય પૃથ્વીની સપાટી પર ગુરુત્વાકર્ષણને કારણે થતા પ્રવેગને અનુરૂપ છે.
                      • \n\t
                    • ગુરુત્વાકર્ષણને કારણે થતો પ્રવેગ g સ્થાનના આધારે થોડો બદલાય છે પરંતુ સામાન્ય રીતે અંદાજે 9.8 m/s² લેવામાં આવે છે.
                      • \n\t
                    • આ મૂલ્યનો ઉપયોગ ભૌતિક વિજ્ઞાનના વિવિધ પ્રશ્નોમાં ગુરુત્વાકર્ષણ બળની ગણતરી કરવા માટે થાય છે.
                      • \n
                    \n\n

                    Additional Information

                    \n\n
                      \n\t
                    • ગુરુત્વાકર્ષણને કારણે પ્રવેગ (Acceleration due to gravity):\n\n\t
                        \n\t\t
                      • તે પૃથ્વીના ગુરુત્વાકર્ષણ બળને કારણે વસ્તુના વેગમાં થતા વધારાનો દર છે.
                        • \n\t\t
                      • તેને g દ્વારા દર્શાવવામાં આવે છે અને તેનું પ્રમાણભૂત મૂલ્ય 9.8 m/s² છે.
                        • \n\t
                      \n\t
                      • \n\t
                    • g માં થતો ફેરફાર:\n\t
                        \n\t\t
                      • ઊંચાઈ સાથે g ઘટે છે અને પૃથ્વીના આકારને કારણે ધ્રુવો તરફ વધે છે.
                        • \n\t\t
                      • તે જે-તે સ્થાન પર પૃથ્વીના પોપડાની ઘનતા પર પણ આધાર રાખે છે.
                        • \n\t
                      \n\t
                      • \n\t
                    • વસ્તુનું વજન:\n\t
                        \n\t\t
                      • વજન એ દળ (m) અને ગુરુત્વાકર્ષણને કારણે પ્રવેગ (g) નો ગુણાકાર છે.
                        • \n\t\t
                      • તે એક બળ છે અને તેને ન્યૂટન (N) માં માપવામાં આવે છે.

                    ಸರಿಯಾದ ಉತ್ತರ 9.8 m/s²(ಆಯ್ಕೆ 2).

                    \n\n

                    Key Points

                    \n\n
                      \n\t
                    • ವಸ್ತುವಿನ ಮೇಲಿನ ಗುರುತ್ವಾಕರ್ಷಣ ಬಲವನ್ನು F = m × g ಎಂಬ ಸೂತ್ರದಿಂದ ನೀಡಲಾಗುತ್ತದೆ.
                      • \n\t
                    • ಇಲ್ಲಿ, F = 49 N ಮತ್ತು m = 5 kg, ಹಾಗಾಗಿ g = F/m = 49/5 = 9.8 m/s².
                      • \n\t
                    • g ನ ಈ ಮೌಲ್ಯವು ಭೂಮಿಯ ಮೇಲ್ಮೈಯಲ್ಲಿನ ಗುರುತ್ವ ವೇಗೋತ್ಕರ್ಷಕ್ಕೆ ಅನುಗುಣವಾಗಿರುತ್ತದೆ.
                      • \n\t
                    • ಗುರುತ್ವ ವೇಗೋತ್ಕರ್ಷದ ಮೌಲ್ಯವು ಸ್ಥಳಕ್ಕೆ ಅನುಗುಣವಾಗಿ ಸ್ವಲ್ಪ ಬದಲಾಗುತ್ತದೆ ಆದರೆ ಸಾಮಾನ್ಯವಾಗಿ ಇದನ್ನು 9.8 m/s² ಎಂದು ಅಂದಾಜಿಸಲಾಗುತ್ತದೆ.
                      • \n\t
                    • ವಿವಿಧ ಭೌತಶಾಸ್ತ್ರದ ಸಮಸ್ಯೆಗಳಲ್ಲಿ ಗುರುತ್ವಾಕರ್ಷಣ ಬಲವನ್ನು ಲೆಕ್ಕಾಚಾರ ಮಾಡಲು ಈ ಮೌಲ್ಯವನ್ನು ಬಳಸಲಾಗುತ್ತದೆ.

                    சரியான விடை 9.8 m/s²(விருப்பம் 2).

                    \n\n

                    Key Points

                    \n\n
                      \n\t
                    • ஒரு பொருளின் மீதான ஈர்ப்பு விசை F = m × g என்ற சூத்திரத்தால் வழங்கப்படுகிறது.
                      • \n\t
                    • இங்கே, F = 49 N மற்றும் m = 5 kg, எனவே g = F/m = 49/5 = 9.8 m/s².
                      • \n\t
                    • இந்த g-இன் மதிப்பு பூமியின் மேற்பரப்பில் ஈர்ப்பினால் ஏற்படும் முடுக்கத்திற்கு ஒத்திருக்கிறது.
                      • \n\t
                    • ஈர்ப்பினால் ஏற்படும் முடுக்கத்தின் மதிப்பு இருப்பிடத்தைப் பொறுத்து சற்று மாறுபடும், ஆனால் பொதுவாக 9.8 m/s² என தோராயமாக எடுத்துக் கொள்ளப்படுகிறது.
                      • \n\t
                    • பல்வேறு இயற்பியல் கணக்குகளில் ஈர்ப்பு விசையை கணக்கிட இந்த மதிப்பு பயன்படுத்தப்படுகிறது.

                        • \n\t\t

                    \n\na0:Te82,\n

                    Concept:

                    \n\n

                    To implement a given logic function using a minimum number of basic gates, we can transform the expression using De Morgan's Laws to eliminate unsupported operations (like the OR operation) so that it only contains multiplications (AND) and inversions (NOT).

                    \n\n

                    De Morgan's Law states that:

                    \n\n

                    \\(\\overline{X + Y} = \\overline{X} \\cdot \\overline{Y}\\)

                    \n\n

                    For a function with multiple inputs, a double inversion is applied to keep the value identical while rewriting the operations:

                    \n\n

                    \\(F = \\overline{\\overline{F}}\\)

                    \n\n

                    Calculation:

                    \n\n

                    Given:

                    \n\n

                    Logic function: F = ABC + DE

                    \n\n

                    Allowed gates: Only 2-input AND gates and NOT gates.

                    \n\n

                    Step 1: Convert the function using De Morgan's Laws

                    \n\n

                    Apply double inversion to remove the OR operation:

                    \n\n

                    \\(F = \\overline{\\overline{ABC + DE}}\\)

                    \n\n

                    Applying De Morgan's Law inside the first inversion yields:

                    \n\n

                    \\(F = \\overline{(\\overline{ABC}) \\cdot (\\overline{DE})}\\)

                    \n\n

                    Step 2: Gate Counting AnalysisA circular loop of radius r is placed in a uniform magnetic field \\( \\vec{B} \\). If the plane of the loop is perpendicular to the field, the magnetic flux through the loop is ______.", "options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "<span>2&pi;r&sup2;B</span>"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "<span>2&pi;rB</span>"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "<span>Br</span>"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "<span>&pi;r&sup2;B</span>"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}, "hn": {"value": "r त्रिज्या का एक वृत्ताकार लूप एकसमान चुंबकीय क्षेत्र \\( \\vec{B} \\) में रखा गया है। यदि लूप का तल क्षेत्र के लंबवत है, तो लूप से गुजरने वाला चुंबकीय फ्लक्स ______ है।", "options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "2πr²B"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "2πrB"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "Br"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "πr²B"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "4", "solution_html": {"en": "\n

                    The correct answer is πr²B(Option 4).

                    \n\n

                    Key Points

                    \n\n
                      \n\t
                    • The formula for magnetic flux is given by Φ = B × A × cos(θ), where:
                      • \n\t
                    • B is the magnetic field, A is the area of the loop, and θ is the angle between the field and the normal to the loop.
                      • \n\t
                    • For a circular loop, the area is A = πr², where r is the radius of the loop.
                      • \n\t
                    • If the plane of the loop is perpendicular to the magnetic field, then θ = 0°, and cos(θ) = 1.
                      • \n\t
                    • Therefore, the magnetic flux becomes Φ = B × πr².
                      • \n\t
                    • Given the options, the correct answer is πr²B (Option 4).
                      • \n
                    \n\n

                    Additional Information

                    \n\n
                      \n\t
                    • Magnetic Flux:\n\n\t
                        \n\t\t
                      • Magnetic flux is a measure of the quantity of magnetism, taking into account the strength and extent of the magnetic field.
                        • \n\t\t
                      • It is represented by Φ and is measured in Weber (Wb).
                        • \n\t
                      \n\t
                      • \n\t
                    • Applications of Magnetic Flux:\n\t
                        \n\t\t
                      • It is used in the design of electromagnetic devices such as motors, generators, and transformers.
                        • \n\t\t
                      • Magnetic flux plays a key role in electromagnetic induction, which is the principle behind the working of electric generators.
                        • \n\t
                      \n\t
                      • \n
                    ", "hn": "\n

                    सही उत्तर πr²B(विकल्प 4) है।

                    \n\n

                    Key Points

                    \n\n
                      \n\t
                    • चुंबकीय फ्लक्स का सूत्र Φ = B × A × cos(θ) द्वारा दिया जाता है, जहाँ:
                      • \n\t
                    • B चुंबकीय क्षेत्र है, A लूप का क्षेत्रफल है, और θ क्षेत्र और लूप के अभिलंब के बीच का कोण है।
                      • \n\t
                    • एक वृत्ताकार लूप के लिए, क्षेत्रफल A = πr² होता है, जहाँ r लूप की त्रिज्या है।
                      • \n\t
                    • यदि लूप का तल चुंबकीय क्षेत्र के लंबवत है, तो θ = 0°, और cos(θ) = 1 होता है।
                      • \n\t
                    • इसलिए, चुंबकीय फ्लक्स Φ = B × πr² हो जाता है।
                      • \n\t
                    • दिए गए विकल्पों के अनुसार, सही उत्तर πr²B (विकल्प 4) है।
                      • \n
                    \n\n

                    Additional Information

                    \n\n

                      The correct answer is 15W (Option 3).

                      \n\n

                      \n\n
                        \n\t

                        • \n\t

                        • \n\t

                        The correct answer is Other branches keep working.

                        \n\n

                        Key Points

                        \n\n
                          \n
                        • Parallel connections are a type of electrical circuit configuration.
                          • \n
                        • In a parallel circuit, if one resistor breaks (open circuit), the other branches keep functioning.
                          • \n
                        • Voltage across each branch in a parallel circuit remains constant.
                          • \n
                        • The current is divided among the branches, but failure in one branch does not impact the others.
                          • \n
                        • This is one reason parallel connections are commonly used in household wiring systems.
                          • \n
                        • A break in one branch does not stop the entire circuit.
                          • \n
                        \n\n

                        Additional Information

                        \n\n
                          \n
                        • Series Connection:\n
                            \n
                          • In a series connection, all components are connected end-to-end.
                            • \n
                          • If one component breaks, the entire circuit stops functioning.
                            • \n
                          \n
                          • \n
                        • Advantages of Parallel Connection:\n
                            \n
                          • Each appliance or branch operates independently.
                            • \n
                          • If one branch fails, it does not affect the other branches.
                            • \n
                          • Voltage across each branch is the same as the source voltage.
                            • \n
                          \n
                          • \n
                        • Applications of Parallel Circuits:\n
                            \n
                          • Used in household electrical wiring.
                            • \n
                          • Common in automotive lighting systems.
                            • \n

                            The correct answer is More charge carriers are generated(Option 3).

                            \n\n

                            Key Points

                            \n\n
                              \n\t
                            • Resistivity of a semiconductor is inversely related to the number of charge carriers.
                              • \n\t
                            • When temperature increases, the energy supplied to the semiconductor increases.
                              • \n\t
                            • This leads to breaking of covalent bonds and generation of more electron-hole pairs.
                              • \n\t
                            • The increase in charge carriers reduces the semiconductor’s resistivity.
                              • \n\t
                            • Unlike conductors, where resistivity increases with temperature, semiconductors exhibit a decrease in resistivity due to this behavior.
                              • \n
                            \n\n

                            Additional Information

                            \n\n
                              \n\t
                            • Band Gap:\n\n\t
                                \n\t\t
                              • The band gap of a semiconductor remains constant for a specific material and does not increase with temperature.
                                • \n\t\t
                              • However, higher temperatures provide energy for electrons to jump the band gap, generating more carriers.
                                • \n\t
                              \n\t
                              • \n\t
                            • Lattice Vibrations:\n\t
                                \n\t\t
                              • Lattice vibrations increase with temperature but do not stop entirely.
                                • \n\t\t
                              • Lattice vibrations primarily affect conductivity in metals rather than in semiconductors.
                                • \n\t
                              \n\t
                              • \n\t
                            • Electrons Lose Energy:\n\t
                                \n\t\t
                              • Electrons gain energy at higher temperatures rather than losing it, enabling them to move to the conduction band.
                                • \n\t
                              \n\t
                              • \n

                              The correct statement is: Option 2: It does not contain internal pull-up resistors.

                              \n\n

                              The primary architectural distinction of Port 0 is its open-drain design. While Ports 1, 2, and 3 have internal pull-up resistors, Port 0's outputs are open-drain and require an external pull-up resistor to output a stable high (logic '1') .

                              \n\n

                              Additional Information:

                              \n\n
                                \n\t
                              • \n\t

                                Option 1 (It is reserved only for memory expansion): This is not entirely accurate. While Port 0 can serve as the multiplexed low-order address and data bus for external memory access, it is also fully functional as a general-purpose I/O port .

                                \n\t
                                • \n\t
                              • \n\t

                                Option 3 (It cannot function as an output port): This is incorrect. Port 0 can function as an output port, but with the added requirement of an external pull-up to produce a high output .

                                \n\t
                                • \n\t
                              • \n\t

                                Option 4 (It is not bit-addressable): This is incorrect. Like the other ports, Port 0 is fully bit-addressable, meaning each of its 8 pins can be controlled individually

                                \n\t
                                • \n

                              \n\n

                              पोर्ट 0 का प्राथमिक संरचनात्मक अंतर इसका ओपन-ड्रेन डिज़ाइन है। जबकि पोर्ट 1, 2, और 3 में आंतरिक पुल-अप प्रतिरोधक होते हैं, पोर्ट 0 के आउटपुट ओपन-ड्रेन होते हैं और एक स्थिर हाई (लॉजिक '1') आउटपुट करने के लिए एक बाहरी पुल-अप प्रतिरोधक की आवश्यकता होती है।

                                  The correct answer is 1.0 N(Option 1).

                                  \n\n

                                  Key Points

                                  \n\n
                                    \n
                                  • Force on a current-carrying conductor in a magnetic field is given by the formula:
                                    • \n
                                  • F = BIL sin θ, where:
                                    • \n
                                      \n
                                    • B = Magnetic field strength (in Tesla).
                                      • \n
                                    • I = Current flowing through the conductor (in Amperes).
                                      • \n
                                    • L = Length of the conductor (in meters).
                                      • \n
                                    • θ = Angle between the conductor and the magnetic field.
                                      • \n
                                    \n
                                  • In this case, B = 0.2 T, I = 5 A, L = 1 m, and the conductor is perpendicular to the magnetic field, so θ = 90°.
                                    • \n
                                  • The formula simplifies to: F = BIL (since sin 90° = 1).
                                    • \n
                                  • Substituting the values: F = (0.2) × (5) × (1) = 1.0 N.
                                    • \n
                                  • Hence, the force acting on the conductor is 1.0 N.
                                    • \n
                                  \n\n

                                  Additional Information

                                  \n\n
                                    \n
                                  • Magnetic Force:\n
                                      \n
                                    • The magnetic force on a current-carrying conductor is a direct result of the interaction between the magnetic field and the moving charges in the conductor.
                                      • \n
                                    • This force is perpendicular to both the magnetic field and the direction of the current.
                                      • \n
                                    \n
                                    • \n
                                  • Applications of Magnetic Force:\n
                                      \n
                                    • Used in devices such as electric motors, generators, and loudspeakers.
                                      • \n
                                    • It is also the principle behind the working of railguns and maglev trains.
                                      • \n
                                    \n
                                    • \n

                                    The correct answer is 8 ms(Option 2).

                                    \n\n

                                    Key Points

                                    \n\n
                                      \n\t
                                    • Time/div knob is set to 2 ms/div.
                                      • \n\t
                                    • The positive half cycle of the periodic triangular signal covers 2 divisions on the X-axis.
                                      • \n\t
                                    • For a periodic signal, the time period is calculated as T = 2 × Time for one half cycle.
                                      • \n\t
                                    • Here, time for one division is 2 ms, and the positive half cycle occupies 2 divisions.
                                      • \n\t
                                    • Time for one half cycle = 2 divisions × 2 ms = 4 ms.
                                      • \n\t
                                    • Total time period T = 2 × 4 ms = 8 ms.

                                        • \n\t
                                      \n\t
                                      • \n\t

                                        \n\t\t
                                      • A signal that repeats itself at regular intervals of time.

                                      \n\n
                                        \n\t
                                      • Ohm’s law states that the current passing through a conductor is directly proportional to the voltage across its ends, provided physical conditions such as temperature remain constant.
                                        • \n\t
                                      • If the temperature of the conductor changes, the resistivity of the material changes, and Ohm’s law may not hold true.
                                        • \n\t
                                      • Ohm’s law is applicable only to ohmic materials such as metals, which exhibit a linear V-I relationship.
                                        • \n\t
                                      • When the material is non-ohmic, such as semiconductors or diodes, the V-I graph is nonlinear, and Ohm’s law does not apply.
                                        • \n\t
                                      • In metallic conductors, temperature changes significantly affect resistance and thus violate Ohm’s law.
                                        • \n
                                      \n\n

                                      Additional Information

                                      \n\n
                                        \n\t
                                      • Factors Affecting Ohm’s Law:\n\n\t
                                          \n\t\t
                                        • Temperature: An increase in temperature increases resistance in metallic conductors, making the V-I relationship nonlinear.
                                          • \n\t\t
                                        • Type of Material: Non-ohmic materials like semiconductors and insulators do not obey Ohm’s law.
                                          • \n\t
                                        \n\t
                                        • \n\t
                                      • Ohmic and Non-Ohmic Materials:\n\t
                                          \n\t\t
                                        • Ohmic materials: Metals such as copper and aluminum follow a linear V-I relationship.
                                          • \n\t\t
                                        • Non-ohmic materials: Devices like diodes and transistors exhibit nonlinear behavior.
                                          • \n\t
                                        \n\t
                                        • \n\t
                                      • Applications of Ohm’s Law:\n\t
                                          \n\t\t
                                        • Used to calculate resistance, current, or voltage in electrical circuits.
                                          • \n\t\t
                                        • Essential for designing and analyzing electrical and electronic systems.
                                          • \n\t
                                        \n\t

                                      \n\nb4:Tbf4,\n

                                      The correct answer is ohm-metre.

                                      \n\n

                                      Key Points

                                      \n\n
                                        \n\t
                                      • Resistivity (Specific Resistance) is the property of a material that opposes the flow of electric current.
                                        • \n\t
                                      • It is defined as the resistance of a conductor having unit length and unit cross-sectional area.
                                        • \n\t
                                      • The relation between resistance and resistivity is:
                                        \n\tR = ρL/A
                                        • \n\t
                                      • Therefore,
                                        \n\tρ = RA/L
                                        • \n\t
                                      • Since:\n\t
                                          \n\t\t
                                        • Resistance (R) is measured in ohm (Ω),
                                          • \n\t\t
                                        • Area (A) in m², and
                                          • \n\t\t
                                        • Length (L) in m,
                                          • \n\t
                                        \n\tthe SI unit of resistivity becomes:
                                        \n\tΩ × m²/m = Ω·m (ohm-metre).
                                        • \n\t
                                      • Hence, the correct answer is Option 3 – ohm-metre.
                                        • \n

                                      \n\n
                                        \n\t

                                          \n\n

                                          Key Points

                                          \n\n
                                            \n
                                          • Magnetic flux is a measure of the total magnetic field passing through a given area.
                                            • \n
                                          • It depends on three factors:
                                            • \n
                                              \n
                                            • Area: The size of the surface through which the magnetic field lines pass.
                                              • \n
                                            • Magnitude of the magnetic field (B): The strength of the magnetic field.
                                              • \n
                                            • Orientation: The angle between the magnetic field and the normal to the surface.
                                              • \n
                                            \n
                                          • The magnetic flux is maximum when the magnetic field is perpendicular to the surface.
                                            • \n
                                          • It becomes zero when the magnetic field is parallel to the surface.
                                            • \n
                                          \n\n

                                              \n
                                            • Magnetic field lines are imaginary lines used to represent the direction and strength of a magnetic field.
                                              • \n
                                            • They emerge from the north pole of a magnet and enter the south pole.
                                              • \n
                                            \n
                                            • \n

                                              \n
                                            • It states that a change in magnetic flux through a circuit induces an electromotive force (EMF) in the circuit.

                                            \n\n

                                            Key Points

                                              \n\t

                                              • \n\t

                                              The correct answer is Force acting per unit charge(Electric Field).

                                              \n\n

                                              Key Points

                                              \n\n
                                                \n\t
                                              • Electric field is defined as the force experienced per unit positive charge at a given point.
                                                • \n\t
                                              • It is a vector quantity and is represented by E.
                                                • \n\t
                                              • The formula for electric field is E = F / q, where F is the force experienced and q is the charge.
                                                • \n\t
                                              • Its SI unit is newton per coulomb (N/C).
                                                • \n\t
                                              • Electric fields are created by static charges or time-varying magnetic fields.
                                                • \n\t

                                                The correct answer is The current is perpendicular to the magnetic field(Option 2).

                                                \n\n

                                                Key Points

                                                \n\n
                                                  \n\t
                                                • The force on a current-carrying conductor in a magnetic field is given by F = BIL sin(θ).
                                                  • \n\t
                                                • Here, B is the magnetic field strength, I is the current, L is the length of the conductor, and θ is the angle between the current and the magnetic field.
                                                  • \n\t
                                                • The force is maximum when θ = 90°.
                                                  • \n\t
                                                • This means the force is maximum when the current is perpendicular to the magnetic field.
                                                  • \n\t
                                                • If the current is parallel to the magnetic field (θ = 0°), the force becomes zero as sin(0°) = 0.
                                                  • \n\t
                                                • This principle is the basis for the working of electric motors, where the force on the conductor creates rotational motion.
                                                  • \n
                                                \n\n

                                                Additional Information

                                                \n\n
                                                  \n\t
                                                • Factors affecting the force:\n\n\t
                                                    \n\t\t
                                                  • The strength of the magnetic field (B): A stronger magnetic field results in a greater force.
                                                    • \n\t\t
                                                  • The current in the conductor (I): Higher current leads to a larger force.
                                                    • \n\t\t
                                                  • The length of the conductor in the magnetic field (L): A longer conductor experiences more force.
                                                    • \n\t
                                                  \n\t
                                                  • \n\t
                                                • Applications:\n\t
                                                    \n\t\t
                                                  • This principle is utilized in the design of devices like electric motors, loudspeakers, and galvanometers.
                                                    • \n\t\t
                                                  • It is also applied in technologies like magnetic levitation and particle accelerators.
                                                    • \n\t
                                                  \n\t

                                                  The correct answer is Mass.

                                                  \n\n

                                                  Key Points

                                                  \n\n
                                                    \n\t
                                                  • Mass is a fundamental property of matter.
                                                    • \n\t
                                                  • Unlike weight, mass is independent of gravitational force.
                                                    • \n\t

                                                    • \n\t

                                                    • \n\t
                                                  • Mass is measured in kilograms (kg) or grams (g).
                                                    • \n\t

                                                  The correct answer is Option 1.

                                                  \n\n

                                                  Key Points

                                                  \n\n
                                                    \n
                                                  • For an ideal half-wave rectifier, the diode conducts during one half of the input sinusoidal cycle and blocks during the other half.
                                                    • \n
                                                  • When the diode orientation is reversed, the output voltage is inverted, but the average value of the diode voltage \\( v_D(t) \\) remains unchanged.
                                                    • \n
                                                  • The average value of \\( v_D(t) \\) becomes zero only under specific conditions, such as reversing the diode orientation.
                                                    • \n
                                                  • Other options (2, 3, and 4) are not universally true for the given system and its behavior.
                                                    • \n
                                                  \n\n

                                                  Additional Information

                                                  \n\n
                                                    \n
                                                  • Half-Wave Rectification:\n
                                                      \n
                                                    • Half-wave rectifiers allow only one half-cycle of the AC voltage to pass, blocking the other half.
                                                      • \n
                                                    • The output voltage is unidirectional and pulsating.
                                                      • \n
                                                    \n
                                                    • \n
                                                  • Diode Voltage:\n
                                                      \n
                                                    • The diode voltage \\( v_D(t) \\) is the potential difference across the diode, which changes during conduction and blocking states.
                                                      • \n
                                                    • It is non-zero during conduction and approximately the source voltage when the diode blocks.
                                                      • \n
                                                    \n
                                                    • \n
                                                  • Ideal Diode Assumption:\n
                                                      \n
                                                    • The ideal diode has zero forward voltage drop and infinite resistance in reverse bias.
                                                      • \n
                                                    • This simplifies analysis but deviates slightly from real-world behavior.
                                                      • \n

                                                      • \n
                                                    • जब डायोड का अभिविन्यास उलट दिया जाता है, तो आउटपुट वोल्टेज उल्टा हो जाता है, लेकिन डायोड वोल्टेज \\( v_D(t) \\) का औसत मान अपरिवर्तित रहता है।
                                                      • \n
                                                    • \\( v_D(t) \\) का औसत मान केवल विशिष्ट परिस्थितियों में शून्य हो जाता है, जैसे कि डायोड के अभिविन्यास को उलटना।
                                                      • \n
                                                    • अन्य विकल्प (2, 3, और 4) दी गई प्रणाली और उसके व्यवहार के लिए सार्वभौमिक रूप से सत्य नहीं हैं।

                                                      \n
                                                    • हाफ-वेव रेक्टिफिकेशन:\n

                                                        • \n
                                                      • आउटपुट वोल्टेज एकदिशीय और स्पंदित होता है।
                                                        • \n
                                                      \n
                                                      • \n
                                                    • डायोड वोल्टेज:\n
                                                        \n
                                                      • डायोड वोल्टेज \\( v_D(t) \\) डायोड के सिरों पर विभवांतर है, जो चालन और अवरुद्ध अवस्थाओं के दौरान बदलता है।
                                                        • \n

                                                    The correct answer is Electric Generator(Option 3).

                                                    \n\n

                                                    Key Points

                                                    \n\n
                                                      \n\t
                                                    • Electric Generator is a device that converts mechanical energy into electrical energy through electromagnetic induction.
                                                      • \n\t
                                                    • In an electric generator, a conductor (like a coil of wire) moves in a magnetic field to produce electricity.
                                                      • \n\t
                                                    • It works on the principle of electromagnetic induction, discovered by Michael Faraday.
                                                      • \n\t
                                                    • Generators are widely used for producing electricity in power plants, industrial facilities, and portable devices.
                                                      • \n\t
                                                    • They are essential in ensuring power supply during outages or in areas where electricity is not readily available.
                                                      • \n\t
                                                    • Unlike batteries, generators do not store energy but directly convert mechanical energy into electrical energy.
                                                      • \n\t
                                                    • Generators can be powered by various energy sources such as steam, water, wind, or internal combustion engines.
                                                      • \n
                                                    \n\n

                                                    Additional Information

                                                    \n\n
                                                      \n\t
                                                    • Battery:\n\n\t
                                                        \n\t\t
                                                      • A battery is a device that stores chemical energy and converts it into electrical energy.
                                                        • \n\t\t
                                                      • It is used in portable electronic devices and vehicles.
                                                        • \n\t
                                                      \n\t
                                                      • \n\t
                                                    • Electric Motor: \n\t
                                                        \n\t\t
                                                      • An electric motor converts electrical energy into mechanical energy.
                                                        • \n\t\t
                                                      • It is used in appliances like fans, washing machines, and industrial machinery.
                                                        • \n\t
                                                      \n\t
                                                      • \n\t
                                                    • Capacitor:\n\t
                                                        \n\t\t
                                                      • A capacitor stores electrical energy in an electric field.
                                                        • \n\t\t
                                                      • It is used in electronic circuits for energy storage, filtering, and signal processing.
                                                        • \n\t

                                                    सही उत्तर विद्युत जनरेटर(विकल्प 3) है।

                                                    \n\n

                                                    Key Points

                                                    \n\n
                                                      \n\t
                                                    • विद्युत जनरेटर एक ऐसा उपकरण है जो विद्युत चुम्बकीय प्रेरण के माध्यम से यांत्रिक ऊर्जा को विद्युत ऊर्जा में परिवर्तित करता है।
                                                      • \n\t
                                                    • विद्युत जनरेटर में, बिजली पैदा करने के लिए एक चालक (जैसे तार की कुंडली) चुंबकीय क्षेत्र में घूमता है।
                                                      • \n\t
                                                    • यह माइकल फैराडे द्वारा खोजे गए विद्युत चुम्बकीय प्रेरण के सिद्धांत पर कार्य करता है।
                                                      • \n\t
                                                    • जनरेटर का उपयोग बिजली संयंत्रों, औद्योगिक सुविधाओं और पोर्टेबल उपकरणों में बिजली पैदा करने के लिए व्यापक रूप से किया जाता है।
                                                      • \n\t
                                                    • वे बिजली कटौती के दौरान या उन क्षेत्रों में बिजली आपूर्ति सुनिश्चित करने के लिए आवश्यक हैं जहां बिजली आसानी से उपलब्ध नहीं है।
                                                      • \n\t
                                                    • बैटरी के विपरीत, जनरेटर ऊर्जा को संग्रहीत नहीं करते हैं बल्कि सीधे यांत्रिक ऊर्जा को विद्युत ऊर्जा में परिवर्तित करते हैं।
                                                      • \n\t
                                                    • जनरेटर को विभिन्न ऊर्जा स्रोतों जैसे भाप, पानी, हवा या आंतरिक दहन इंजन द्वारा संचालित किया जा सकता है।
                                                      • \n
                                                    \n\n

                                                    Additional Information

                                                    \n\n
                                                      \n\t
                                                    • बैटरी:\n\n\t
                                                        \n\t\t
                                                      • बैटरी एक ऐसा उपकरण है जो रासायनिक ऊर्जा को संग्रहीत करता है और इसे विद्युत ऊर्जा में परिवर्तित करता है।
                                                        • \n\t\t
                                                      • इसका उपयोग पोर्टेबल इलेक्ट्रॉनिक उपकरणों और वाहनों में किया जाता है।
                                                        • \n\t
                                                      \n\t
                                                      • \n\t
                                                    • विद्युत मोटर: \n\t
                                                        \n\t\t
                                                      • विद्युत मोटर विद्युत ऊर्जा को यांत्रिक ऊर्जा में परिवर्तित करती है।
                                                        • \n\t\t
                                                      • इसका उपयोग पंखे, वाशिंग मशीन और औद्योगिक मशीनरी जैसे उपकरणों में किया जाता है।
                                                        • \n\t
                                                      \n\t
                                                      • \n\t
                                                    • संधारित्र:\n\t
                                                        \n\t\t
                                                      • संधारित्र विद्युत क्षेत्र में विद्युत ऊर्जा को संग्रहीत करता है।
                                                        • \n\t\t
                                                      • इसका उपयोग इलेक्ट्रॉनिक सर्किट में ऊर्जा भंडारण, फ़िल्टरिंग और सिग्नल प्रोसेसिंग के लिए किया जाता है।
                                                        • \n\t

                                                    \n\n
                                                      \n\t
                                                    • విద్యుత్ జనరేటర్ అనేది విద్యుదయస్కాంత ప్రేరణ ద్వారా యాంత్రిక శక్తిని విద్యుత్ శక్తిగా మార్చే పరికరం.
                                                      • \n\t
                                                    • విద్యుత్ జనరేటర్‌లో, విద్యుత్తును ఉత్పత్తి చేయడానికి ఒక వాహకం (వైర్ కాయిల్ వంటివి) అయస్కాంత క్షేత్రంలో కదులుతుంది.
                                                      • \n\t
                                                    • ఇది మైకేల్ ఫెరడే కనుగొన్న విద్యుదయస్కాంత ప్రేరణ సూత్రంపై పనిచేస్తుంది.
                                                      • \n\t
                                                    • విద్యుత్ ప్లాంట్లు, పారిశ్రామిక సౌకర్యాలు మరియు పోర్టబుల్ పరికరాలలో విద్యుత్తును ఉత్పత్తి చేయడానికి జనరేటర్లు విస్తృతంగా ఉపయోగించబడతాయి.
                                                      • \n\t
                                                    • విద్యుత్ అంతరాయం ఏర్పడినప్పుడు లేదా విద్యుత్ అందుబాటులో లేని ప్రాంతాలలో విద్యుత్ సరఫరాను నిర్ధారించడంలో ఇవి అవసరం.
                                                      • \n\t
                                                    • బ్యాటరీల వలె కాకుండా, జనరేటర్లు శక్తిని నిల్వ చేయవు కానీ నేరుగా యాంత్రిక శక్తిని విద్యుత్ శక్తిగా మారుస్తాయి.
                                                      • \n\t
                                                    • జనరేటర్లు ఆవిరి, నీరు, గాలి లేదా అంతర్గత దహన యంత్రాల వంటి వివిధ శక్తి వనరుల ద్వారా శక్తిని పొందుతాయి.

                                                      సరైన సమాధానం విద్యుత్ జనరేటర్(ఆప్షన్ 3).

                                                      \n\n
                                                        \n\t
                                                      • విద్యుత్ జనరేటర్ అనేది విద్యుదయస్కాంత ప్రేరణ ద్వారా యాంత్రిక శక్తిని విద్యుత్ శక్తిగా మార్చే పరికరం.
                                                        • \n\t
                                                      • విద్యుత్ జనరేటర్‌లో, విద్యుత్తును ఉత్పత్తి చేయడానికి ఒక వాహకం (వైర్ కాయిల్ వంటివి) అయస్కాంత క్షేత్రంలో కదులుతుంది.
                                                        • \n\t
                                                      • ఇది మైకేల్ ఫెరడే కనుగొన్న విద్యుదయస్కాంత ప్రేరణ సూత్రంపై పనిచేస్తుంది.
                                                        • \n\t

                                                        • \n\t
                                                      • విద్యుత్ అంతరాయం ఏర్పడినప్పుడు లేదా విద్యుత్ అందుబాటులో లేని ప్రాంతాలలో విద్యుత్ సరఫరాను నిర్ధారించడంలో ఇవి అవసరం.
                                                        • \n\t
                                                      • బ్యాటరీల వలె కాకుండా, జనరేటర్లు శక్తిని నిల్వ చేయవు కానీ నేరుగా యాంత్రిక శక్తిని విద్యుత్ శక్తిగా మారుస్తాయి.
                                                        • \n\t
                                                      • జనరేటర్లు ఆవిరి, నీరు, గాలి లేదా అంతర్గత దహన యంత్రాల వంటి వివిధ శక్తి వనరుల ద్వారా శక్తిని పొందుతాయి.

                                                          • \n\t\t
                                                        • ఇది ఫ్యాన్లు, వాషింగ్ మెషీన్లు మరియు పారిశ్రామిక యంత్రాల వంటి పరికరాలలో ఉపయోగించబడుతుంది.

                                                          • \n\t\t
                                                        • ఇది ఎలక్ట్రానిక్ సర్క్యూట్లలో శక్తి నిల్వ, ఫిల్టరింగ్ మరియు సిగ్నల్ ప్రాసెసింగ్ కోసం ఉపయోగించబడుతుంది.
